    Notes:                                                                                     附註：
    (1)    Net profit margin is profit for the year divided by revenue.                        (1) 淨利潤率指年內溢利除收入。
    (2)    Return on total assets is profit for the year divided by average total assets.      (2) 總資產回報率指年內溢利除平均資產總額。
    (3)    Return on capital employed is the ratio of profit before income tax expense         (3) 淨資本回報率指利息及稅項前盈利除總資產減流動負債。
           and interest divided by total assets less current liabilities.
    (4)    Liabilities-to-EBITDA ratio is the ratio of total liabilities divided by adjusted   (4)    負債對 EBITDA 倍數指總負債除經調整利息、稅項、折舊
           EBITDA.                                                                                    及攤銷前盈利。
    (5)    Debts-to-net assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing               (5)    淨資產負債率指計息借貸、可轉換債券與融資租賃承擔
           borrowings, convertible bonds and obligations under finance leases                         之和除以總權益。
           divided by total equity.
    (6)    Gearing ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings,               (6)    資本負債率指計息借貸、可轉換債券與融資租賃承擔之
           convertible bonds and obligations under finance leases divided by the                      和除以總權益、計息借貸、可轉換債券與融資租賃承擔
           total equity, interest-bearing borrowings, convertible bonds and obligations               之和。
           under finance leases.
    (7)    Debts-to-assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings,       (7)    資產負債率指計息借貸、可轉換債券與融資租賃承擔之
           convertible bonds and obligations under finance leases divided by total                    和除以總資產。
           assets.
    (8)    Average finance cost is the ratio of aggregated amount of each category of          (8)    平均融資成本指各種類借貸成本乘其佔總借貸比例，然
           borrowing multiply by its interest rate divided by the total borrowing.                    後加總。
    (9)    The dividends payable shall be paid in HK$.                                         (9)    應付股息將以港元支付。
    (10)   Due to business combinations under common control completed in 2017,                (10)   由於二零一七年完成共同控制下的業務合併，本集團的
           the financial information of the Group has been restated to include the                    財務資料已重列，按合併基準包括中石油京唐液化天然
           financial position and results of operation of PetroChina Jingtang LNG Co.,                氣有限公司（「京唐公司」）的財務狀況及業績。
           Ltd. (“Jingtang Co.”) on a combined basis.
    (11)   The financial information of the Group has not been restated for the                (11)   本集團的財務資料並未就收購京唐公司而重列，原因是
           acquisition of Jingtang Co. as the directors consider that the unrestated                  董事認為，未重列的財務資料更適合對本集團業務經營
           financial information is more appropriate for year-on-year comparison of                   的變動按年進行比較。
           the change in the Group’s business operation.
    (12)   The financial information of the Group has not been restated for the                (12)   本集團的財務資料並未就收購京唐公司及中石油昆侖燃
           acquisition of Jingtang Co. and PetroChina Kunlun Gas Co., Ltd. (“Kunlun                   氣有限公司（「昆侖燃氣」）而重列，原因是董事認為，未
           Gas”) as the directors consider that the unrestated financial information                  重列的財務資料更適合對本集團業務經營的變動按年進
           is more appropriate for year-on-year comparison of the change in the                       行比較。
           Group’s business operation.
    (13)   Core profit before income tax expense is defined as profit before                   (13)   除所得稅費用前核心溢利指不包括物業、廠房及設備、
           income tax expense excluding impairment losses on property, plant                          無形資產、應收賬款及其他應收款項減值虧損及匯兌虧
           and equipment, intangible assets, accounts and other receivables and                       損╱收益之除所得稅費用前溢利。
           exchange loss/gain.
    (14)   Core profit attributable to owners of the Company is defined as profit              (14)   本公司股東應佔核心溢利指不包括本公司股東應佔之物
           attributable to owners of the Company excluding impairment losses on                       業、廠房及設備、無形資產、應收賬款及其他應收款項
           property, plant and equipment, intangible assets, accounts and other                       減值虧損及匯兌虧損╱收益及其有關稅務影響之本公司
           receivables and exchange loss/gain attributable to owners of the Company                   股東應佔溢利。
           and its related tax effect.
    (15)   Adjusted EBITDA is defined as profit before income tax expense, excluding           (15)   經調整扣除利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前盈利指不包括物
           impairment loss on property, plant and equipment, impairment loss on                       業、廠房及設備減值虧損、無形資產減值虧損、應收賬
           intangible assets, impairment loss on accounts and other receivables,                      款及其他應收款項減值虧損、匯兌虧損╱收益、利息以
           exchange loss/gain, interest and depreciation, depletion and amortisation.                 及折舊、損耗及攤銷之除所得稅費用前溢利。
    (16)   The Group has initially applied HKFRS 9 and HKFRS 15 at 1 January 2018.             (16)   於二零一八年一月一日，本集團已初步應用香港財務報

The revenue of the Group reached RMB105,470 million,
representing a year-on-year increase of 18.90%; the profit
attributable to owners of the Company was RMB4,634 million,
representing a year-on-year decrease of 2.65%; earnings per
share (basic) were RMB57.41 cents.

本集團實現銷售收入達人民幣 1,054.70 億元，同比增長 18.90%；
本公司股東應佔溢利錄得人民幣 46.34 億元，同比減少 2.65%；
每股盈利（基本）為人民幣 57.41 分。

     BUSINESS REVIEW                                                         業務回顧

     Kunlun Energy Company Limited (the “Company”) and its 昆侖能源有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱
     subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) seized the opportunities 「本集團」）抓住天然氣產業發展契機，充分發揮產
     to expand in the natural gas industry, made full use of the        業結構優勢，持續提升企業價值。
     advantages in its business structure and continued to increase its
     corporate values.

     ANNUAL RESULTS                                                          全年業績

     In 2018, the macro economy of the People’s Republic of China            二零一八年，中華人民共和國（「中國」）宏觀經濟平
     (the “PRC”) remained stable and the environmental policies              穩運行，環保政策助力天然氣市場蓬勃發展。中國
     facilitated the rapid development of the natural gas market. The        政府出台多項環保政策，持續推進大氣污染防治工
     government of the PRC implemented various environmental                 作，強化重點地區的民用、採暖、工業等行業煤改
     policies, continuously propelled the air pollution prevention and       氣，全國天然氣消費量快速增長，繼續保持雙位數
     control work and strengthened the coal-to-gas conversion for            增長態勢。本公司聚焦高質量發展目標，抓住天然
     residential use, heating and industrial use in key areas, through       氣需求旺盛等有利時機，持續深化改革創新，取得
     which national natural gas consumption recorded rapid growth            了良好的經營業績，本公司獲評中國城市燃氣協會
     and continually maintained double-digit growth. The Company             成立 30 週年突出貢獻獎、中國證券金紫荊獎之最佳
     focused on quality development, grasped the favourable                  上市公司。
     opportunities arising from the strong demand for natural gas, and
     continued to push through reforms and innovations, thus achieved
     remarkable operating results. The Company was awarded China
     Gas Association 30th Anniversary Outstanding Contribution
     Award and the Best Listed Company of China Securities Golden
     Bauhinia Awards.

     During the year, with the joint efforts of all employees, the revenue   本年度，在全體員工共同努力下，本集團實現銷售

24   KUNLUN ENERGY COMPANY LIMITED
         Annual Report 2018
Management Discussion
    and Analysis
                                     管理層討論與分析

Kunlun Energy Company Limited and its subsidiaries continued to develop its
natural gas business segment during the year ended 31 December 2018. Profit
before income tax expense of the Group for the Year was approximately RMB11,985
million, representing an increase of 4.40% as compared with RMB11,480 million
for the last year. Profit attributable to owners of the Company for the Year was
approximately RMB4,634 million, representing a decrease of 2.65% as compared
with RMB4,760 million for the last year.

昆侖能源有限公司及其附屬公司於截至二零一八年十二月三十一日止年度繼續擴大
天然氣業務板塊。本集團於本年度之除所得稅費用前溢利約為人民幣 11,985 百萬
元，比去年金額人民幣 11,480 百萬元增加 4.40%。於本年度本公司股東應佔溢利
約為人民幣 4,634 百萬元，比去年金額人民幣 4,760 百萬元減少 2.65%。
                                                         Management Discussion and Analysis

     The board of directors (the “Board”) of Kunlun Energy Company             昆侖能源有限公司（「本公司」及其附屬公司統稱「本
     Limited (the “Company”, and together with its subsidiaries, the           集團」）董事會（「董事會」）欣然於截至二零一八年
     “Group”) is pleased to present this Corporate Governance Report           十二月三十一日止年度（「本年度」）本公司年報內呈
     in the Company’s annual report for the year ended 31 December             報此份企業管治報告。
     2018 (the “Year”).

     The Company recognises the importance of good corporate                   本公司深明良好企業管治對本公司健全發展之重要
     governance to the Company’s healthy growth and has devoted                性，並已致力尋求及制定切合其業務需要之企業管
     considerable efforts to identifying and formulating corporate             治常規。
     governance practices appropriate to the needs of its business.

     The Company’s corporate governance practices are based                    本公司之企業管治常規乃建基於香港聯合交易所有
     on the principles (the “Principles”), code provisions (the “Code          限公司（「聯交所」）證券上市規則（「上市規則」）附錄
     Provisions”) and certain recommended best practices (the                  14 所載企業管治守則（「企業管治守則」）內所列之原
     “Recommended Best Practices”) as set out in the Corporate                 則（「原則」）、守則條文（「守則條文」）及若干建議最
     Governance Code (the “CG Code”) contained in Appendix 14                  佳常規（「建議最佳常規」）而釐定。
     to the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock
     Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) (the
     “Listing Rules”).

     The Company has applied the Principles and the Code Provisions            本公司於本年度已應用企業管治守則所載之原則及
     as set out in the CG Code and complied with all the Code                  守則條文，並遵守所有守則條文。
     Provisions throughout the Year.

     The Company has also put in place certain Recommended Best                本公司亦已實施企業管治守則所載之若干建議最佳
     Practices as set out in the CG Code.                                      常規。

     The Company periodically reviews its corporate governance                 本公司定期檢討其企業管治常規以確保該等常規持
     practices to ensure that these continue to meet the requirements          續符合企業管治守則之規定，並確認其董事會於為
     of the CG Code, and acknowledges the important role of its                本公司業務提供有效領導及方向，以及確保本公司
     Board in providing effective leadership and direction to Company’s        營運之透明度及問責性上之重要角色。
     business, and in ensuring transparency and accountability of
     Company’s operations.

The Board takes responsibility for all major matters of the                董事會負責本公司一切重大事務，包括批准及監察
Company, including the approval and monitoring of all policy               一切政策事宜、整體策略及預算、內部監控及風險
matters, overall strategies and budgets, internal control and risk         管理系統、重大交易（尤其涉及利益衝突者）、財務
management systems, material transactions (in particular those             資料、委任董事及其他重大財務及經營事宜。
which may involve conflict of interests), financial information,
appointment of Directors and other significant financial and
operational matters.

All Directors have full and timely access to all relevant information      為確保董事會程序及所有適用規則及規例均獲得遵
as well as the advice and services of the Company Secretary, with          守，全體董事均有充足及適時之途徑取得一切有關
a view to ensuring that Board procedures and all applicable rules          資料及公司秘書之意見及服務。
and regulations are followed.

Each Director is normally able to seek independent professional            經向董事會提出要求，每名董事一般可在適當情況
advice in appropriate circumstances at the Company’s expense,              下尋求獨立專業意見，費用由本公司支付。
upon making request to the Board.

The day-to-day management, administration and operation of                 本公司之日常管理、行政及營運已轉授予行政總裁
the Company are delegated to the Chief Executive Officer and               及高級管理人員。已授權之職能及工作目標乃定期
the senior management. The delegated functions and work tasks              作出檢討。上述行政人員訂立任何重大交易前須獲
are periodically reviewed. Approval has to be obtained from the            得董事會批准。

management to discharge their responsibilities.                            面支持。

back and obtain prior approval from the Board before making

management.

Board Diversity Policy (the “Board Diversity Policy”) to align the       配合董事會成員多元化。
board diversity.

Practices and Conduct of Meetings                                      會議常規及操守

Notices of regular Board meetings are served to all Directors          董事會定期會議通知至少於會議前 14 日向全體董事
at least 14 days before the meetings and reasonable notice             發出，而其他董事會會議一般會發出合理通知。委
is generally given for other Board meetings. For committee             員會會議將根據有關職權範圍所訂之所須通知期發
meetings, notices are served in accordance with the required           出通知。
notice period stated in the relevant terms of reference.

Agenda and Board papers together with all appropriate, complete        議程及會議文件連同所有適當、完備及可靠資料至
and reliable information are normally sent to all Directors/           少於各董事會會議或委員會會議舉行前三天送交全
committee members at least 3 days before each Board meeting            體董事╱委員會成員，以令董事知悉本公司之最近
or committee meeting to keep the Directors abreast of the latest       發展及財政狀況，並能夠在掌握有關資料之情況下
developments and financial position of the Company and to              作出決定。需要時，董事會及每名董事亦有個別及
enable them to make informed decisions. The Board and each             獨立途徑接觸高級管理人員。
Director also have separate and independent access to the senior
management whenever necessary.

The Chairman, Chief Executive Officer, President, Chief Financial      需要時，主席、行政總裁、總裁、財務總監及公司
Officer and Company Secretary will attend Board and committee          秘書將出席董事會及委員會會議，以就本公司之業
meetings, when necessary, to advise on business developments,          務發展、財務及會計事宜、法規遵守、企業管治及
financial and accounting matters, statutory compliance, corporate      其他重要方面提供意見。
governance and other major aspects of the Company.

Minutes of all Board meetings and committee meetings are kept          所有董事會會議及委員會會議之會議記錄由公司秘
by the Company Secretary. Minutes are prepared after each              書備存。會議紀錄於各會議後編製，而最後定稿則
meeting and the final version is signed by the Chairman or the         由主席或相關委員會主席（視情況而定）簽署，並於
chairman of the relevant committees (as the case may be) and           下一次董事會會議由董事會或於一下次委員會會議
confirmed by the Board in the following Board Meeting or by the        由相關委員會（視情況而定）確認。經確認之會議紀

       financial statements or, if such disclosures are inadequate,        的結論是基於核數師報告日止所取得的審計
       to modify our opinion. Our conclusions are based on the             憑證。然而，未來事項或情況可能導致 貴
       audit evidence obtained up to the date of our auditor’s             集團不能持續經營。
       report. However, future events or conditions may cause the
       Group to cease to continue as a going concern.

     3    SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                              3   主要會計政策

    (i)   Leased assets                                                (i)   租賃資產

          An arrangement, comprising a transaction or a                      若包含單一或連串交易的一項安排，
          series of transactions, is or contains a lease if the              按其實質內容而不是其法律形式而言
          Group determines that the arrangement conveys a                    可按單一次或連續付款形式在安排期

          period of time in return for a payment or a series of              定工作乃以評估該項安排的實質內容

          an evaluation of the substance of the arrangement                  的法律形式。
          and is regardless of whether the arrangement takes
          the legal form of a lease.

                      Write-off policy                                                 撇銷政策

                      The gross carrying amount of a                                   倘若沒有實際可回收的
                      financial asset is written off (either                           前景，金融資產的賬面

                      there is no realistic prospect of                                撇銷。一般情況下，撇

                      the debtor does not have assets                                  現金流的資產或收入來

                      generate sufficient cash flows to repay

                      recognised as a reversal of impairment                           確認為減值撥回。

                        –     Calculation of recoverable amount                          –   計算可收回金額

                              The recoverable amount of an asset                             資產之可收回金額是公
                              is the greater of its fair value less                          平值減銷售成本與使用
                              costs of disposal and value in use. In                         價值兩者之較高者。在
                              assessing value in use, the estimated                          評估使用價值時，估計
                              future cash flows are discounted to                            未來現金流量按可反映
                              their present value using a pre-tax                            當時市場對貨幣時間值
                              discount rate that reflects current                            之估計及該資產之特定
                              market assessments of the time                                 風險之稅前折現率折現
                              value of money and the risks specific                          至其現值。凡資產所產
                              to the asset. Where an asset does                              生之現金流入基本上並
                              not generate cash inflows largely                              非獨立於其他資產所產
                              independent of those from other                                生之現金流入，則就獨
                              assets, the recoverable amount is                              立地產生現金流入之最
                              determined for the smallest group of                           小 資 產 組 別（ 即 現 金 產
                              assets that generates cash inflows                             生單位）來釐定可收回
                              independently (i.e. a cash-generating                          金額。
                              unit).

                        –     Recognition of impairment losses                           –   確認減值虧損

                              An impairment loss is recognised in                             當資產或其所屬現金產
                              profit or loss if the carrying amount                           生單位之賬面值高於其
                              of an asset, or the cash-generating                             可收回金額時，便會在
                              unit to which it belongs, exceeds                               損益中確認減值虧損。
                              its recoverable amount. Impairment                              就現金產生單位確認之
                              losses recognised in respect of cash-                           減值虧損會予以分配，
                              generating units are allocated first                            首先降低分配至現金產
                              to reduce the carrying amount of                                生單位（或一組單位）之
                              any goodwill allocated to the cash-                             任何商譽之賬面值，然
                              generating unit (or group of units)                             後 按 比 例 降 低 單 位（ 或
                              and then, to reduce the carrying                                一組單位）中其他資產
                              amount of the other assets in the                               之賬面值，但資產賬面
                              unit (or group of units) on a pro rata                          值不會降至低於其個別
                              basis, except that the carrying value                           公平值減去銷售成本
                              of an asset will not be reduced below                          （如可計量）或使用價值
                              its individual fair value less costs of                        （如可確定）。
                              disposal (if measurable) or value in

    (n)   Convertible bonds                                              (n)   可轉換債券

                 convertible bonds is measured at fair value based                  分按公平值根據未來利息及本金還款

                 discounted at the prevailing market rate of interest               市場利率折現。權益部分為整體可轉

                 component is the difference between the initial                    始公平值之間之差額。有關發行複合
                 fair value of the convertible bonds as a whole                     金融工具的交易成本按所得款項的比
                 and the initial fair value of the liability component.             例分配至負債及權益部分。
                 Transaction costs that relate to the issue of a
                 compound financial instrument are allocated to the
                 liability and equity components in proportion to the
                 allocation of proceeds.

                 The liability component is subsequently carried at                 負債部分其後按攤銷成本列賬。就負
                 amortised cost. Interest expense recognised in profit              債部分於損益內確認的利息開支乃使
                 or loss on the liability component is calculated using             用實際利率法計算。權益部分會於可
                 the effective interest method. The equity component                轉換債券權益儲備確認，直至債券被

                 until either the bonds are converted or redeemed.

                 redeemed, the convertible bond equity reserve is

    (p)   Accounts payable                                              (p)   應付賬款

          Accounts payable are recognised initially at fair                   應付賬款初步按公平值確認，其後採
          value and subsequently measured at amortised cost                   用實際利率法按已攤銷成本計量。
          using the effective interest method.

    (q)   Borrowings                                                    (q)   借貸

          Borrowings are measured initially at fair value less                借貸初步按公平值減交易成本計量。
          transaction costs. Subsequent to initial recognition,               於初步確認後，計息借貸則採用實際
          interest-bearing borrowings are stated at amortised                 利率法按攤銷成本列賬。
          cost using the effective interest method.



          General and specific borrowing costs directly                       直接來自收購、興建或生產合資格資
          attributable to the acquisition, construction or                    產且該等資產需經較長時間方會達至
          production of qualifying assets, which are assets                   預定可使用或出售狀態之一般及特定
          that necessarily take a substantial period of time to               借貸成本，計入該等資產之成本，直
          get ready for their intended use or sale, are added                 至大致上達至其預定可使用或出售狀
          to the cost of those assets, until such time as the                 態為止。
          assets are substantially ready for their intended use
          or sale.

          All other borrowing costs are recognised in the profit              所有其他借貸成本於其產生期間於損
          or loss in the period in which they are incurred.                   益中確認。

          Borrowings are classified as current liabilities                    借貸乃分類為流動負債，除非本集團
          unless the Group has unconditional rights to defer                  擁有無條件權利可延後償付負債至於
          settlements of the liabilities for at least 12 months               報告期末後至少 12 個月則作別論。
          after the end of the reporting period.

    (r)   Taxation                                                      (r)   稅項

          Income tax expense for the period comprises                         期間所得稅費用包括當期及遞延稅
          current and deferred tax. Tax is recognised in the                  項。稅項於損益內確認，惟如與於其
          profit or loss, except to the extent that it relates to             他全面收益或直接於權益確認之項目
          items recognised in other comprehensive income                      有關，稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或
          or directly in equity. In this case, the tax is also                直接於權益中確認。
          recognised in other comprehensive income or
          directly in equity, respectively.

           (r)   Taxation (Continued)                                          (r)   稅項（續）

                 The current income tax charge is calculated on                      當期所得稅支出乃根據本集團、聯營
                 the basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively                  公司及合資企業經營所在並產生應課
                 enacted at the statement of financial position date                 稅收入之國家於結算日已實行或大致
                 in the countries where the Group, associates and                    實行之稅法計算。管理層就適用稅務
                 joint ventures operate and generate taxable income.                 法例詮釋所規限之情況定期評估報稅
                 Management periodically evaluates positions taken                   表之狀況，並在適用情況下根據預期
                 in tax returns with respect to situations in which                  須向稅務機關支付之稅款確定撥備。
                 applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation.
                 It establishes provisions where appropriate on the
                 basis of amounts expected to be paid to the tax
                 authorities.

                 Deferred income tax is recognised, using the                        遞延所得稅乃採用負債法就資產負債
                 liability method, on temporary differences arising                  之稅基與彼等於綜合財務報表之賬面
                 between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and                 值兩者之暫時差異予以確認。然而，

                 statements. However, deferred tax liabilities are not               譽，則不予確認。倘初步確認一項交
                 recognised if they arise from initial recognition of                易（業務合併除外）中之資產及負債於
                 goodwill. The deferred income tax is not accounted                  交易時產生之遞延所得稅既不影響會
                 for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset               計處理亦不影響應課稅溢利或虧損，
                 or liability in a transaction other than a business                 則此遞延所得稅不予入賬。遞延所得
                 combination that at the time of the transaction                     稅乃採用於財政年度結算日前已實行
                 affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss.              或大致實行並預期於相關遞延所得稅
                 Deferred income tax is determined using tax rates                   資產變現或遞延所得稅負債清償時適
                 (and laws) that have been enacted or substantively                  用之稅率（及稅法）釐定。
                 enacted by the statement of financial position date
                 and are expected to apply when the related deferred
                 income tax asset is realised or the deferred income
                 tax liability is settled.

                 Deferred income tax assets are recognised only                      遞延所得稅資產僅當未來應課稅溢利
                 to the extent that it is probable that future taxable               很有可能與可動用之暫時差異抵銷時
                 profit will be available against which the temporary                方予確認。
                 differences can be utilised.

                 differences arising on investments in subsidiaries,                 司及合資企業產生之暫時差異計提撥

                 increase in the provision due to passage of time is

      5    CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                         5   關鍵會計估計及判斷
           JUDGEMENTS
           Estimates and judgements are regularly evaluated by the                       本集團定期根據過往經驗及其他因素（包括
           Group, based on historical experience and other factors                       對未來事件在當前情況下認為合理之預期）
           which include expectations of future events that are                          對估計及判斷進行評估。
           believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.

           Key sources of estimation uncertainty and judgement are                       估計不明朗因素及判斷之主要來源載述下
           described as follows:                                                         文：
           (a)   Estimation of oil and natural gas reserves                              (a)   石油及天然氣儲量之估計
                 Estimates of oil and natural gas reserves are key                             石油及天然氣儲量之估計對本集團之
                 elements in the Group’s investment decision-making                            投資決策程序至關重要，同時亦是進
                 process. They are also an important element in                                行減值測試之重要因素。探明石油及
                 testing for impairment. Changes in proved oil and                             天然氣儲量之變化，尤其是探明已開
                 natural gas reserves, particularly proved developed                           發儲量，將影響於本集團綜合財務報
                 reserves, will affect unit-of-production depreciation,                        表就與石油及天然氣生產活動相關之
                 depletion and amortisation recorded in the Group’s                            物業、廠房及設備所記錄之單位產量
                 consolidated financial statements for property, plant                         折舊、損耗及攤銷。探明已開發儲量
                 and equipment related to oil and gas production                               之 減 少 將 增 加 折 舊、 損 耗 及 攤 銷 金
                 activities. A reduction in proved developed reserves                          額。探明儲量估計可根據新資料作出
                 will increase depreciation, depletion and amortisation                        向上或向下修訂，例如，來自開發鑽
                 charges. Proved reserve estimates are subject to                              探及生產活動或來自經濟因素之變化
                 revision, either upward or downward, based on new                             之新資料，包括產品價格、合同條款
                 information, such as from development drilling and                            或開發計劃等。
                 production activities or from changes in economic
                 factors, including product prices, contract terms or
                 development plans.

          are reviewed for possible impairments whenever                      賬面值可能無法收回時予以檢討是否
          events or changes in circumstances indicate that                    可能減值。確定資產是否減值及減值
          the carrying amount may not be recoverable.                         之金額涉及管理層之估計及判斷，例
          Determination as to whether and how much an                         如原油及天然氣之未來價格，貼現預
          asset is impaired involves management estimates                     計現金流所用之貼現率及出產情況。
          and judgements such as future prices of crude oil                   然而，減值檢討及計算乃根據與本集

          However, the impairment reviews and calculations                    於該等年度對任何資產進行減值，而

          the Group’s business plans. Favourable changes
          to some assumptions may allow the Group to

      34   OIL PRODUCTION SHARING CONTRACT                                34   油田產品分成合同

           Leng Jiapu Contract                                                 冷家堡合同

           Pursuant to the Leng Jiapu Contract signed in 1997, the             根據簽訂於一九九七年的冷家堡合同，本集
           Group agreed to acquire 70% of the production sharing               團同意收購 70% 之產品分成權益，作價人民
           interest for RMB1,008 million and to fund its share of              幣 1,008 百萬元，並負擔於冷家堡合同所界
           cost of the development carried out for the realisation             定之地區（「冷家堡合同地區」）內就生產石油
           of oil production (the “Development Operations”) in the             產品（「開發作業」）應佔之開發成本，首兩年
           area as defined in the Leng Jiapu Contract (the “LJP                開發期之估計成本為 65.5 百萬美元（約人民
           Contract Area”), at an estimated cost of US$65.5 million            幣 474 百萬元），此外再負責首兩年後 70%
           (approximately RMB474 million) in the first two years of the        之開發成本，以取得冷家堡合同地區 70% 之
           development period and be further responsible for 70% of            產油量。
           the development cost after the first two years, in exchange
           for a 70% share in the oil production from the LJP Contract
           Area.

           Pursuant to the Leng Jiapu Contract, the Group shall bear           根據冷家堡合同，本集團負擔冷家堡合同地
           70% of the costs required for the Development Operations            區內開發作業及生產分成所需之 70% 成本，
           in the LJP Contract Area which shall be allocated (after            產量分配為（經扣除當地稅項及企業所得稅
           deduction of local taxes and enterprise income tax) firstly         後）先用於收回經營成本，其餘由本集團與
           towards operating costs recovery and thereafter in the              中國石油按 70% 與 30% 之比例攤分，作為
           proportion of 70% to the Group and 30% to PetroChina                收回投資成本及賺取的利潤。
           towards investment recovery and profit.

           The Leng Jiapu Contract provides twenty consecutive                 冷家堡合同規定，由開始商業作業日期起計
           years of production sharing from the date of                        連續分佔石油產量二十年並將於二零二三年
           commencement of commercial production and will expire               十二月三十一日屆滿。
           on 31 December 2023.

           In connection with the Leng Jiapu Contract, the Group               本集團亦就冷家堡合同與一個由中國石油集
           has also entered into an Entrustment Contract with an               團全資擁有並營運之業務實體訂立委託合
           operational entity owned and operated by CNPC, whereby              同，據此，後者獲委託承擔作為作業者的責
           the latter is entrusted to take up the responsibility as            任。根據委託合同，已成立聯合開發管理組
           an operator. Under the Entrustment Contract, a Joint                織，履行在作業者合同項下之責任。
           Development Management Organisation was established
           for the performance of the contractual responsibilities
           under the operatorship.

      36   SEGMENT INFORMATION                                           36   板塊資料

           Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent             營運板塊之呈報方式與向主要營運決策者提
           with the internal reporting provided to the chief operating        供之內部報告貫徹一致，乃由本公司執行董
           decision maker which is determined as the Executive                事決定。
           Directors of the Company.

           The Group organises its business around products and               本集團按產品及服務組織其業務。就產品及
           services. From the products and services perspective, the          服務而言，本集團廣泛從事一系列石油相關
           Group is engaged in a broad range of petroleum related             業務，其收入來自四個營運板塊：勘探與生
           activities and derives its revenue from its four operating         產、天然氣銷售、LNG 加工與儲運及天然氣
           segments: Exploration and Production, Natural Gas Sales,           管道。
           LNG Processing and Terminal and Natural Gas Pipeline.

           The Exploration and Production segment is engaged in               勘探與生產板塊從事原油及天然氣之勘探、
           the exploration, development, production and sales of              開發、生產和銷售。天然氣銷售板塊從事天
           crude oil and natural gas. The Natural Gas Sales segment           然氣不同產品及 LPG 的批發與零售。LNG 加
           is engaged in the wholesales and retail sales of various           工與儲運板塊從事 LNG 加工、卸載、儲存、
           natural gas and LPG products. LNG Processing and                   氣化及裝車。天然氣管道板塊從事通過管道
           Terminal segment is engaged in the processing, unloading,          輸送天然氣業務。
           storing, gasification and entrucking of LNG. Natural Gas
           Pipeline segment is engaged in the transmission of natural
           gas through pipeline.

           The Executive Directors assess the performance of the              執行董事根據各板塊之除所得稅費用、應佔
           operating segments based on each segment’s profit/(loss)           聯營公司及合資企業之溢利減虧損前之溢
           before income tax expense, share of profits less losses of         利╱（虧損）評估經營板塊之表現（「板塊業
           associates and joint ventures (“segment results”).                 績」）。

           Segment assets exclude deferred and current taxes,                 板塊資產不包括遞延及即期稅項、其他金融
           other financial assets, investments in associates and joint        資產、於聯營公司及合資企業之投資（即「板
           ventures (“segment assets”), as all of which are managed           塊資產」），因以上各項均集中管理。
           on a central basis.

           Corporate income and expenses, net, mainly refers to               公司收支淨額主要指現金及現金等值項目所
           interest income earned from cash and cash equivalents,             賺取的利息收入、匯兌收益╱虧損淨額、公
           net exchange gains/losses, general and administrative              司層面產生之一般性和管理費用及利息支
           expenses and interest expenses incurred at corporate               出。
           level.

           Corporate assets mainly comprise cash and cash                     公司資產主要包括公司層面所持有之現金及
           equivalents held at corporate level.                               現金等值項目。

     2017 business combination under common control                     二零一七年共同控制下之業務合併

     As mentioned in Note 2(b) to these consolidated financial          誠如該等綜合財務報表附註 2(b) 所述，收購
     statements, the acquisition of Jingtang Co. has been               京唐公司根據會計指引第 5 號列賬。因此，
     accounted for in accordance with AG 5. Accordingly,                本集團所收購的京唐公司的資產及負債按歷
     the assets and liabilities of Jingtang Co. acquired by the         史成本列賬，及本集團於合併前的綜合財務
     Group have been accounted for at historical cost and the           報表已經重列，以包括京唐公司的財務狀況
     consolidated financial statements of the Group for periods         及經營業績（以綜合基準）。本集團最終支付
     prior to the combination have been restated to include the         的代價人民幣1,906百萬元已視為股權交易。
     financial position and results of operation of Jingtang Co.
     on a combined basis. The final consideration of RMB1,906
     million payable by the Group has been treated as an equity
     transaction.

     HKFRS 16, Leases                                                          香港財務報告準則第 16 號，租賃

              certainty of the engineering analysis on which the project                士及實體（包括政府實體）批准進行開發。
              or program was based; and (B) The project has been
              approved for development by all necessary parties and
              entities, including governmental entities.

      (v)     Existing economic conditions include prices and costs             (v)     現有的經濟條件包含確定一個油氣藏經濟生
              at which economic producibility from a reservoir is to be                 產能力的價格和成本。除非由合同約定，該
              determined. The price shall be the average price during                   價格是指在本報告期截止日以前的十二個月
              the 12-month period prior to the ending date of the period                的算術平均價格，乃確定為該期間內每個月
              covered by the report, determined as an unweighted                        第一天價格的未加權算術平均數，但不包括
              arithmetic average of the first-day-of-the-month price for                基於未來條件做出的價格調整。
